How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Loadouts/UI

# Locations
Aliases: Foraging Islands, Island, Islands, Limbo, Location, Public Island, Public Islands, Zone, Zones
[Locations]
A map ([[Map (Hub)]]) of the SkyBlock islands, as shown in the [[Village]].
Locations are places in the SkyBlock world where players collect items, level up [[Skills]] and [[Collections]], kill [[Mob|Mobs]], interact with [[NPC|NPCs]], complete [[Quests]] and [[Chapters]], and more. Each location has a name that can be seen at any time on the player's scoreboard.
Locations are often times categorized by [[Skills]], with multiple locations existing for each main skill. For example, the [[Combat]] skill has multiple islands themed around it, including the [[Spider's Den]], [[The End]], the [[Crimson Isle]], and more. Some locations, however, are not tied to particular skills. The [[Hub Island]] is the first island players access after their [[Private Island]], and features activities centered around many skills.
Some locations also have access requirements. For example, [[The Park]] requires the player to reach [[Foraging|Foraging I (1)]] and complete [[Lumber Jack]]'s quest, while the [[Moonglade Marsh]] requires [[Foraging|Foraging XII (12)]]. Additionally, [[The End]] requires [[Combat|Combat XII (12)]], but the [[Void Sepulture]] within it requires [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] to be accessed. Some areas also have level recommendations, such as the [[Ruins]], which warns the player if they do not have [[Combat|Combat III (3)]] or higher.
Some locations are also instanced, meaning when the player visits it, they will either not encounter any other players, or only players in their party can join. These location types include the [[Private Island]], [[The Garden]], the [[Catacombs]], [[Kuudra's Hollow]], and [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
Players can unlock [[Fast Travel]], [[Travel Scrolls]], and [[Portals]] to more quickly access locations. Many locations also have [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST|Soundtracks]] that play in the background while in them.
## Private Locations
### Private Island
The default [[Private Island]]
The [[Private Island]] is where all players begin their SkyBlock adventure. Players can build and break blocks freely here, allowing them to customize the island to their liking. They can also place [[Minions]] to harvest resources. The default starting island contains a [[Cobblestone Minion|Cobblestone Minion I]] and a [[Portals|Portal]] to the [[Hub Island]].
### The Garden
[[The Garden]] is an island where players can farm. Unlocked at [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 5]] and after speaking to [[Sam]], it is similar to the [[Private Island]] in that each player has their own and they can build on it.
## General Locations
### Hub Island
The [[Hub Island]]
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Village]]|The spawn point and center area of the [[Hub Island]]. Splits off into other areas focused on specific [[Skills]].|[[Agnes]], [[Alcina]], [[Alixer]], [[Amelia]], [[Baker]], [[Bingo (NPC)]], [[Christopher]], [[Chuckleton]], [[Fear Mongerer]], [[Fisherwoman Enid]], [[Guy]], [[Hoppity]], [[Hub Selector]], [[Hypixel Discord]], [[Jerry]], [[Lukas the Aquarist]], [[Piggy]], [[Romero]], [[Salesman]], [[Torrhus Canyon (NPC)]]|
|^ [[Abiphones & Co.]]|Allows the player to purchase basic [[Abiphones]].|[[Alda]], [[Karis]]|
|^ [[Auction House]]|Allows the player to bid on and purchase items sold by other players.|[[Auction Agent]], [[Auction Master]]|
|^ [[Bank]]|Features the [[Banker]], who allows the player to store their [[Coins]].|[[Banker]]|
|^ [[Bazaar Alley]]|Allows the player to access the [[Bazaar]] to purchase commodity items.|[[Bazaar (NPC)]], [[Bazaar Agent]]|
|^ [[Builder's House]]|Features merchants who sell blocks for building.|[[Builder]], [[Mad Redstone Engineer]], [[Wool Weaver]]|

|^ [[Flower House]]|Features [[Marco]], who talks about [[Sprays]].|[[Marco]]|
|^ [[Canvas Room]]|Allows the player to paint images.|[[Marco]]|
|^ [[Museum]]|Serves as a place to store items in exchange for SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Curator]], [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth III]]|
|^ [[Hexatorum]]|Contains [[The Handler]], who can upgrade the player's items.|[[The Handler]]|
|^ [[Pet Care]]|Features various Pet ([[Pets]])-related NPCs ([[NPC]]).|[[Bea]], [[Fann]], [[George]], [[Kat]], [[Zog]]|

|^ [[Taylor's Shop]]|Allows the player to purchase Cosmetics from [[Taylor]].|[[Taylor]]|

|^ [[Archery Range]]|Contains Target Practice ([[Archery Range]]), which unlocks new [[Arrows]] at [[Jax]]'s shop.|[[Jax]]|
|^ [[Library]]|Features the [[Librarian]], who sells [[Enchanting]]-related items.|[[Enchantment Nerd]], [[Librarian]]|
|^ [[Thaumaturgist]]|Allows the player to upgrade [[Accessories]].|[[Combat Merchant]], [[Jacobus]], [[Maths Enjoyer]], [[Maxwell]], [[Ozanne]]|
|^ [[Trade Center]]|Contains shops related to when [[Diaz]] is elected.|[[Liz]], [[Richard]], [[Zarina]]|
|[[Graveyard]]|Features low level zombies.|[[Pat]], [[Romero]]|
|^ [[Tavern]]|Allows the player to purchase [[Brews]] and start [[Slayer]] quests.|[[Bartender]], [[Maddox the Slayer]]|
|^ [[Crypts]]|Features higher level zombies, often used for [[Zombie Slayer]].|None|
|^ [[Catacombs Entrance]]|Allows the player to enter the [[Catacombs]].|[[Malik]], [[Mort]], [[Ophelia]]|
|[[Mining District]]|Introduces the player to [[Mining]].|[[Mining Merchant]]|
|^ Blacksmith ([[Blacksmith (Zone)]])|Serves as a place to Reforge ([[Reforging]]) or apply [[Runes]] to items.|[[Blacksmith]], [[Dusk]], [[Smithmonger]]|
|[[Coal Mine]]|Allows the player to collect [[Coal]] and [[Cobblestone]], as well as travel to the [[Gold Mine]].|None|
|[[Farm]]|Introduces the player to [[Farming]], allowing the player to harvest [[Wheat]] or travel to [[The Barn]].|[[Alchemist]], [[Arthur]], [[Farm Merchant]], [[Farmer Rigby]], [[Ludleth]], [[Shania]], [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]|
|^ [[Farmhouse]]|Serves as a place to farm [[Wheat]], talk to [[Farming]]-related NPCs ([[NPC]]), and access [[The Barn]].|[[Anita]], [[Jacob]]|

|^ [[Fisherman's Hut]]|Contains the [[Ship]], which allows players to access other Fishing islands.|[[Captain Baha]], [[Gavin]], [[Researcher Gilbert]]|
|[[Carnival]]|Contains various minigames that can be played to earn [[Carnival Tokens]].|[[Carnival Cowboy]], [[Carnival Fisherman]], [[Carnival Leader]], [[Carnival Pirateman]], [[Chantelle]], [[Doug]]|
|[[Colosseum]]|Not much here anymore.|[[Romero]]|
|[[Mountain]]|Used for multiple quests.|[[Romero]], [[Scoop]], [[Wizard]]|
|^ [[Weather Station]]|Contains [[Professor Wynd]], who talks about [[Weather]].|[[Professor Wynd]], [[Sunny]]|

#### Moonglade Marsh
The [[Moonglade Marsh]]
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|The first island of [[Galatea]]. Requires Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]) to access.|None|

|^ [[Tangleburg Library]]|A library housing [[Loras]], who talks about the Precursors.|[[Loras]]|
|^ [[Tangleburg's Path]]|A path just outside [[Tangleburg]] leading to [[The Park]], branching off into a small pond.|[[Hina]]|

|[[South Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]). The [[Moonglade Beacon]] and [[Trial of Blue Flames]] can be found here.|[[Boris]], [[Brian]], [[Sawyer]], [[Soul Campfire Adept]], [[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|
|[[South Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tomb Floodway]].|None|
|[[West Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|
|[[Verdant Summit]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|
|[[Westbound Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|None|
|[[Murkwater Outpost]]|A place where players can trade [[Forest Essence]] for various [[Foraging]], [[Hunting]], and other miscellaneous perks.|[[Rosemary]]|
|[[North Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|[[Alan]], [[Kiara]], [[Teddy]], [[Vaeri]]|
|[[North Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Houses the [[Red House]] and leads to the [[Side-Ember Way]].|None|
|^ [[Red House]]|A place where players can donate different variants of The Fish ([[The Fish Family]]) to [[Coral]] in exchange for [[Fishing]] XP, [[Respiration]], and SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Coral]]|
|[[Moonglade's Edge]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and learn how to use [[Huntraps]]. Houses a portal to the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. Also leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|[[Dax]]|
|[[Murkwater Loch]]|The center of the Moonglade Marsh, where players can learn about Agatha's Contests ([[Agatha]]) and forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]).|[[Agatha]], [[Jaeger]]|
|[[Murkwater Shallows]]|A place where players can fish, forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and [[Sea Lumies]].|[[Nemo]]|
|[[Murkwater Depths]]|An underwater area where players can collect [[Sea Lumies]] and Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Water Creatures. Branches off into the [[Forest Temple]] via the [[Ancient Ruins]], [[Drowned Reliquary]] via the [[Reefguard Pass]], the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]] via the [[Dive-Ember Pass]], the [[Driptoad Delve]] via the [[Driptoad Pass]], the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]], the [[Squid Cave]], and the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]).|None|
|[[Ancient Ruins]]|An underwater entrance to the [[Forest Temple]]. Punch the soul lanterns in ascending order of number of [[Sea Lumies]] next to it to open the gate.|[[Tilus]]|
|[[Forest Temple]]|Donate [[Coins]] and Enchanted Fig Logs ([[Enchanted Fig Log]]) to the Forest Starborn to become a benefactor and gain [[Foraging Fortune]].|None|
|[[Reefguard Pass]]|An underwater path to access the [[Drowned Reliquary]].|None|
|[[Drowned Reliquary]]|An underwater ruin containing Tidetots ([[Tidetot]]), Seacurses ([[Seacurse]]), and Hydrospears ([[Hydrospear]]).|None|
|[[Squid Cave]]|An underwater area found on the path to the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]) that contains Lumisquids ([[Lumisquid]]).|None|
|Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]])|A cavern where the [[Jade Dragon]] sells the Jade Dragon Egg ([[Jade Dragon Pet]]).|[[Jade Dragon]]|
|[[Dive-Ember Pass]]|An underwater path to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Stride-Ember Fissure]]|A lava lake where players can hunt Stridersurfers ([[Stridersurfer]]).|None|
|[[Side-Ember Way]]|A bypass that leads to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Pass]]|An underwater path that connects the [[Driptoad Delve]] and the [[Murkwater Depths]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Delve]]|A pond where the player can fish and enter the [[Murkwater Depths]] from the [[Driptoad Pass]].|None|
|[[Kelpwoven Tunnels]]|An underwater tunnel full of coral where the player can hunt Spikes ([[Spike]]).|None|
|[[Bubbleboost Column]]|An underwater vertical tunnel to quickly travel to [[Tangleburg]] from the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]] and [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]].|None|
|[[Wyrmgrove Tomb]]|A place where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Chillshots ([[Chillshot]]) and Chillblades ([[Chillblade]]).|None|

|^ [[Mines of Divan]]|Contains the four Keepers ([[Keepers of Divan]]), who give the [[Metal Detector]] to find the four [[Scavenged Items]]. These can be given back to the Keepers to obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]). Also contains lots of Pure Gold ([[Gold Ingot]]) for players to mine from.|Keeper of Diamond ([[Keepers of Divan]]), Keeper of Emerald ([[Keepers of Divan]]), Keeper of Gold ([[Keepers of Divan]]), Keeper of Lapis ([[Keepers of Divan]])|
|^ Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Crystal Hollows)]])|Rarely appears as a structure in the [[Mithril Deposits]]. Allows the player to purchase a Golden Dragon Egg ([[Golden Dragon Pet]]).|[[Golden Dragon]]|
|^ [[Precursor Remnants]]|One of the five main areas of the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Can be mined through to obtain [[Hard Stone]], Ruby Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]), Sapphire Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]), and more.|[[Chunk]]|
|^ [[Lost Precursor City]]|Appears as a structure in the [[Precursor Remnants]]. Contains [[Professor Robot]], who allows the player to obtain the Sapphire Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) in exchange for all 6 parts dropped by Automatons ([[Automaton]]).|[[Professor Robot]]|
|^ [[Magma Fields]]|One of the five main areas of the [[Crystal Hollows]], appearing throughout the entire area at low Y levels. Can be mined through to obtain [[Hard Stone]], Ruby Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]), Topaz Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]), and more.|None|
|^ [[Khazad-dûm]]|Appears as a structure in the [[Magma Fields]]. Contains [[Bal]], which can be defeated to obtain the Topaz Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]), as well as rarely the [[Bal Pet]].|None|
## Fishing Locations
### Backwater Bayou
The [[Backwater Bayou]]
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Backwater Bayou]]|Accessed from the Ship Navigator ([[Ship]]) in the [[Fisherman's Hut]] after reaching Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]]). Introduces the player to [[Rod Parts]], [[Fishing Hotspots]], multiple early-game [[Sea Creatures]], and more.|[[Captain Baha]], [[Carly]], [[Hattie]], [[Jonesy]], [[Junker Joel]], [[Researcher Gilbert]], [[Roddy]]|

The [[Lotus Atoll]]
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Lotus Atoll]]|Accessed from the Ship Navigator ([[Ship]]) after reaching Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]]). Features [[Trophy Frogs]], the [[Lotus]] collection, [[Sea Creatures]], and more.|[[Captain Baha]], [[Researcher Betty]], [[Researcher Gilbert]], [[Researcher Kay]], [[Researcher Lily]], [[Researcher Ribery]], [[Researcher Timmy]], [[Researcher Tina]]|

## Unreleased Locations
- [[Lunarise]] - An upcoming [[Foraging]]-based location, and the final island of [[Galatea]].
- [[Pale Citadel]] - An upcoming [[Combat]]-based location.
- [[Desecrated Shrine]] - An upcoming [[Fishing]]-based location.
## Removed Locations
- [[Floating Islands]] - was replaced with [[The Park]].
- [[Blazing Fortress]] - was replaced with [[Crimson Isle]].
- [[Barter Bank Show]] - was replaced while on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] with the [[Empty Bank]].
- [[Snowball Fight Cave]] - was part of [[Jerry's Workshop]].
- [[Operation Mayhem]] - was part of [[Jerry's Workshop]].
- [[Lonely Island]] - was engulfed by the [[Viking Longhouse]] when [[The Park]] was revamped.
- [[Weaponsmith]] - was engulfed by the [[Combat Settlement]] when the [[Hub Island]] was revamped.
## Trivia
- It is possible to travel to other islands with an [[Aspect of the End]] or other items, even if that island is locked, as long as the player has enough [[Mana]].
  - However, traveling too far into the island will teleport the player back to spawn, with a chat message saying that you cannot travel too far from the island.

|^ [[Tavern]]|Allows the player to purchase [[Brews]] and start [[Slayer]] quests.|[[Bartender]], [[Maddox the Slayer]]|
|^ [[Crypts]]|Features higher level zombies, often used for [[Zombie Slayer]].|None|

# Locations/Moonglade Marsh
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|The first island of [[Galatea]]. Requires Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]) to access.|None|
|[[Tangleburg]]|A village near the front of the island, where one can learn the basics of [[Foraging]] and [[Hunting]].|[[Albert]], [[David Hunterborough]], [[Oden]], [[Pounce]], [[Salvage Old Attributes]], [[Swoop]], [[Talia]]|
|^ [[Fusion House]]|A place where players can fuse Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) through the Fusion Machine ([[Attribute Fusion]]).|[[Kysha]]|
|^ [[SwampCut Inc.]]|A shop where one can obtain the [[Seriously Damaged Axe]], [[Decent Axe]], and [[Canopy Armor]].|[[Amaury]]|
|^ [[Tangleburg Bank]]|A bank that allows players to access the Banking System ([[Bank]]).|[[Banker Bonsai]]|
|^ [[Tangleburg Library]]|A library housing [[Loras]], who talks about the Precursors.|[[Loras]]|
|^ [[Tangleburg's Path]]|A path just outside [[Tangleburg]] leading to [[The Park]], branching off into a small pond.|[[Hina]]|
|[[Evergreen Plateau]]|A large plateau where players first learn how to use [[Lassos]] and Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Forest Creatures. Several large Fig Trees can be found here, where players can forage for Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|[[Auryon]]|
|[[South Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]). The [[Moonglade Beacon]] and [[Trial of Blue Flames]] can be found here.|[[Boris]], [[Brian]], [[Sawyer]], [[Soul Campfire Adept]], [[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|
|[[South Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tomb Floodway]].|None|
|[[West Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|
|[[Verdant Summit]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|
|[[Westbound Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|None|
|[[Murkwater Outpost]]|A place where players can trade [[Forest Essence]] for various [[Foraging]], [[Hunting]], and other miscellaneous perks.|[[Rosemary]]|
|[[North Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|[[Alan]], [[Kiara]], [[Teddy]], [[Vaeri]]|
|[[North Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Houses the [[Red House]] and leads to the [[Side-Ember Way]].|None|
|^ [[Red House]]|A place where players can donate different variants of The Fish ([[The Fish Family]]) to [[Coral]] in exchange for [[Fishing]] XP, [[Respiration]], and SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Coral]]|
|[[Moonglade's Edge]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and learn how to use [[Huntraps]]. Houses a portal to the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. Also leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|[[Dax]]|
|[[Murkwater Loch]]|The center of the Moonglade Marsh, where players can learn about Agatha's Contests ([[Agatha]]) and forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]).|[[Agatha]], [[Jaeger]]|
|[[Murkwater Shallows]]|A place where players can fish, forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and [[Sea Lumies]].|[[Nemo]]|
|[[Murkwater Depths]]|An underwater area where players can collect [[Sea Lumies]] and Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Water Creatures. Branches off into the [[Forest Temple]] via the [[Ancient Ruins]], [[Drowned Reliquary]] via the [[Reefguard Pass]], the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]] via the [[Dive-Ember Pass]], the [[Driptoad Delve]] via the [[Driptoad Pass]], the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]], the [[Squid Cave]], and the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]).|None|
|[[Ancient Ruins]]|An underwater entrance to the [[Forest Temple]]. Punch the soul lanterns in ascending order of number of [[Sea Lumies]] next to it to open the gate.|[[Tilus]]|
|[[Forest Temple]]|Donate [[Coins]] and Enchanted Fig Logs ([[Enchanted Fig Log]]) to the Forest Starborn to become a benefactor and gain [[Foraging Fortune]].|None|
|[[Reefguard Pass]]|An underwater path to access the [[Drowned Reliquary]].|None|
|[[Drowned Reliquary]]|An underwater ruin containing Tidetots ([[Tidetot]]), Seacurses ([[Seacurse]]), and Hydrospears ([[Hydrospear]]).|None|
|[[Squid Cave]]|An underwater area found on the path to the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]) that contains Lumisquids ([[Lumisquid]]).|None|
|Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]])|A cavern where the [[Jade Dragon]] sells the Jade Dragon Egg ([[Jade Dragon Pet]]).|[[Jade Dragon]]|
|[[Dive-Ember Pass]]|An underwater path to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Stride-Ember Fissure]]|A lava lake where players can hunt Stridersurfers ([[Stridersurfer]]).|None|
|[[Side-Ember Way]]|A bypass that leads to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Pass]]|An underwater path that connects the [[Driptoad Delve]] and the [[Murkwater Depths]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Delve]]|A pond where the player can fish and enter the [[Murkwater Depths]] from the [[Driptoad Pass]].|None|
|[[Kelpwoven Tunnels]]|An underwater tunnel full of coral where the player can hunt Spikes ([[Spike]]).|None|
|[[Bubbleboost Column]]|An underwater vertical tunnel to quickly travel to [[Tangleburg]] from the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]] and [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]].|None|
|[[Wyrmgrove Tomb]]|A place where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Chillshots ([[Chillshot]]) and Chillblades ([[Chillblade]]).|None|
|[[Tomb Floodway]]|A place where players can fish up Flexbones ([[Flexbone]]).|[[Martin]]|
|[[Tranquil Pass]]|A tunnel where players can harvest [[Bamboo]].|None|
|[[Tranquility Sanctum]]|A cavern where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Bambuleafs ([[Bambuleaf]]) between Spring and Summer, and Mochibears ([[Mochibear]]) between Autumn and Winter.|None|

# Locations/Mushroom Desert
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Mushroom Desert]]|The second [[Farming]]-based island, which requires Farming V (5) ([[Farming]]) to enter. It serves as place to harvest [[Sand]], [[Cactus]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cocoa Beans]], Red Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]), and Brown Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]), kill Rabbits ([[Rabbit]]), [[Sheep]], and Mushroom Cows ([[Mushroom Cow]]), and go [[Fishing]].|[[Farmer Jon]], [[Hunter Ava]]|
|^ [[Desert Settlement]]|The entrance to the island. Contains lots of [[Sand]] and [[Cactus]], as well as several quest NPCs ([[NPC]]).|[[Beth]], [[Friendly Hiker]], [[Hungry Hiker]], [[Mason]]|
|^ [[Desert Mountain]]|Contains a pathway to [[Jake's House]].|None|
|^ [[Jake's House]]|Contains [[Jake]], who sells [[Animal Axes]] and [[Reforge Stones]] in exchange for bringing him certain creatures.|[[Jake]]|
|^ [[The Observatory]] [AHN] (This item or feature is currently only available on the Alpha Hypixel Network.)|Contains the [[Spaceman]].|[[Spaceman]]|
|^ [[Archaeological Site]]|Contains the [[Treasure Hunter]], who sells hints for various treasure locations in exchange for [[Coins]].|[[Treasure Hunter]]|
|^ [[Oasis]]|Contains [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cocoa Beans]], Rabbits ([[Rabbit]]) and [[Sheep]], and water to go [[Fishing]] from.|None|
|^ [[Shepherd's Keep]]|Contains the [[Shepherd]], who gives the player [[Pelts]] in exchange for bringing him his [[Sheep]]. Also contains [[Sand]] and [[Cactus]].|[[Shepherd]]|
|^ [[Trapper's Den]]|Features [[Talbot]]'s [[Pelts]] shop, [[Tony]]'s Tier XII Farming [[Minions]] shop, [[Trevor]] and his hunting quests, and [[Tammy]], who explains the animals that [[Trevor]] wants hunted.|[[Talbot]], [[Tammy]], [[Tony]], [[Trevor]]|
|^ [[Mushroom Gorge]]|A large area for players to collect Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]) or kill Mushroom Cows ([[Mushroom Cow]]).|[[Hungry Hiker]], [[Romero]]|
|^ [[Overgrown Mushroom Cave]]|Connected to the [[Mushroom Gorge]], and contains more Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]).|None|
|^ [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]|Connected to the [[Mushroom Gorge]], and contains more Mushrooms ([[Mushroom]]). Also contains [[Moby]]'s shop, and a path to the [[Underground Lab]].|[[Moby]]|
|^ [[Underground Lab]]|This location is accessed during [[Beth]]'s Shady Jake ([[Quests]]) quest. It is expanded upon during her Further Investigate Jake ([[Quests]]) quest, which unlocks the [[Crop Analyzer]].|[[Beth]], [[Jake]]|

|^ [[Barry HQ]]|Each protestor in the [[Barry Center]] moves here after their request has been handled.|[[Barry]], [[Creed]], [[Dust]], [[Frankie]], [[Harriette]], [[Soma]], [[Stain]], [[Violetta]]|
|^ [[Empty Bank]]|Not much here.|None|
|^ [[Half-Eaten Cave]]|Serves as a place to primarily obtain Half-Eaten Carrots ([[Half-Eaten Carrot]]).|None|
|^ [[Taylor's]]|Contains Taylor ([[Taylor (Rift)]]), who plays a role in [[Detective Amog]]'s reverse-murder quest, as well as [[Seymour]], who sells [[Exceedingly Comfy Sneakers]].|[[Detective Amog]], [[Joliet]], [[Master Tactician Fink]], [[Ramero]], [[Seymour (Rift)]], [[Taylor (Rift)]]|

|^ [[Déjà Vu Alley]]|Switches between red, green, and yellow blocks. Moving through it on red will teleport the player back to the [[Village Plaza]] and consume [[Rift Time]]. Leads to the [[Living Cave]].|[[Blacksmith (Rift)]], [[Detective Amog]]|
|[[Living Cave]]|Requires the [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]] to enter, and costs 5:00 [[Rift Time]] to do so. Contains Living Metal Snakes, Autonulls ([[Autonull]]), multiple [[Enigma Souls]], and an entry point to the [[Living Stillness]].|None|
|^ [[Living Stillness]]|Primarily contains Frozilles ([[Frozille]]), which can be killed to obtain Frosty Cruxes ([[Frosty Crux]]) for the [[Living Timecharm]].|[[Rock]]|
|Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]])|Requires the [[Living Timecharm]] to enter, and costs 15:00 [[Rift Time]] to do. While in the area, [[Rift Time]] goes down at half the speed. Contains the [[Bacte]] fight, which grants Bacte Fragments ([[Bacte Fragment]]) for the [[Globulate Timecharm]].|[[Mole (NPC)]]|
|[[Barrier Street]]|Requires the [[Globulate Timecharm]] to enter, and costs 10:00 [[Rift Time]] to do so. Contains yearly auctions for items in exchange for [[Motes]], similar to [[Shen's Auction]]. Also leads to the [[Photon Pathway]].|[[Damia (Rift)]]|

# Longswords
Aliases: Longsword
Longswords are a class of [[Swords]] with increased [[Swing Range]].
## Mechanics
A Longsword is any item which is tagged as a Longsword item in its description. There are currently 4 Longswords in the game.
## List of Longswords
|Image|Name|Rarity|Ability|Swing Range|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bone Reaver]]|[[Bone Reaver]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Ability: Reaving Strike - Slash in a huge arc, dealing 125% melee damage to all enemies hit! Charges: 4 / 5s Deals +1% damage to Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) Mobs for every 1% of your missing health.|0.5 [[Swing Range]]|
|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Ability: Reaving Strike - Slash in a huge arc, dealing 135% melee damage to all enemies hit! Charges: 5 / 4s Deals +1% damage for every 1% of missing health on the target.|1 [[Swing Range]]|
|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|(same as above)|1.5 [[Swing Range]]|
|[[Giant's Sword]]|[[Giant's Sword]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Ability: Giant's Slam - Slam your sword into the ground dealing 100,000 damage to nearby enemies. Mana Cost: 100 Cooldown: 30s|1 [[Swing Range]]|
|[[Dark Claymore]]|[[Dark Claymore]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])||2 [[Swing Range]]|
## Trivia
- The only Ultimate Enchantments that Longswords were able to have used to be [[Chimera]] and [[Combo]].
  - This was found to be a bug, and was subsequently patched.

# Loot Sharing
Aliases: Loot Share, Loot share, Loot sharing, Lootshare
Loot Sharing is a mechanic which allows multiple players to receive drops from enemies.
## Mechanics
If multiple players deal 1% of [[Damage]] to a mob, or 10% of [[Damage]] to a [[Slayer]] boss, and are within 30 blocks of the mob when it dies, the top 5 damagers will receive drops from the mob. The message that appears when Loot Sharing works is: LOOT SHARE You received loot for assisting <player>!
Mobs can have unique loot pools for Loot Sharing, which are used instead of their regular loot pools if the player Loot Shares. They usually have lower amounts of items or lower drop chances for rare items. Drops from Loot Share loot pools are rolled based off of the player's [[Magic Find]] and [[Pet Luck]], and are not affected by [[Looting]] or [[Luck]].
Loot Sharing works with all mobs, except on the [[Private Island]] and in [[Dungeons]]. The [[Vanquisher]] does not have a cap on the amount of players that can receive loot from it.
This mechanic was implemented to ease the killing of mobs on the [[Crimson Isle]], as they have multiple millions of [[Health]] there. Loot Sharing was first only implemented for [[Ghost|Ghosts]], however with the 0.13 update, this was changed so that any mob in the game could be loot shared.
## Trivia
- Loot Sharing is effective with [[Phoenix Pet]] drops. This is likely a bug.
- Loot sharing is effective on slayer bosses, meaning you can drop loot from other people's bosses.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Unknown Date|Loot Sharing is effective with nearly every mob on the [[Crimson Isle]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Unknown Date|Loot Sharing is implemented, and only works with Ghosts ([[Ghost]]).|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Loot Sharing implemented across the game.|
|Unknown Date|Loot Sharing is removed in [[Dungeons]] and on the [[Private Island]].|

## Information Gathering
This section is about Information Gathering for this page as a note for Editors, and is not game information.
If you know information of an election on the alpha network, and would like to contribute, you may:
1. If the election UI is currently available in-game, you may copy the UIs (Mayor, and Mayor Election UIs) to the corresponding subpage using wikitools; or
2. Recreate the UI using a {{UI}} syntax to the corresponding subpage; or
3. Write a text description to the corresponding subpage, and provide the details if possible: the election year, the candidate perks/votes/data about the elected mayor, or links to screenshots to relevant data
  "The corresponding subpage" means a subpage of the correct year for your input, for example, [[Mayor Election/Alpha/Year 241-260]].
  Incomplete information is accepted provided that it is true.
If you have any difficulties in the process or do not want to put information on the subpage for any reason, you may put down information on this page's talk page instead. You are also encouraged to find staff assistance here or discuss information in Discord.
Linked from: [[Mayor Election]], [[Mayor Election/Events]]

### Election Year 225
Some time after [[Barry]] was removed as a [[Mayor Election|Regular Candidate]] on the Main Server, the same was done on the Alpha Network. Since the Alpha Network was running 176 days faster than the Main Server at that time and the election has already started, the candidates were reshuffled. Admins might have interfered with the mayor perks, since some candidates have gained or changed perks that is impossible with the [[Mayor Election|Perks Distribution]] system.
Although [[Barry]] was removed from the election, it might have counted people voted Barry before he was removed and did not change vote, which is the highest. The ballot box in the mayor interface does not show Barry's vote count. Barry held the mayor position for one last time on the Alpha Hypixel Network in Year 226, after his final term on the main server in Year 224.
### Election
The following is the candidates and perks after reshuffling.
The following is the election candidates and perks before reshuffling.
### Election
The supposed time during election of Year 224 may have passed when the Alpha Network is closed.
### Election Result
Election result is unknown.
### Election
The Alpha Network may be closed when this election was supposed to happen.

### Election Year 241
When [[Finnegan]] was added as a [[Mayor Election|Regular Candidate]] on the Main Server, this election was reshuffled after this election has ended, and with the addition of Finnegan as a candidate, albeit with 0 votes. With the data given by the API, it was deduced that:
- [[Paul]] was replaced by Finnegan.
- Finnegan was added with all 3 perks.
- The perks of all other candidates were unaffected.
- Candidate positions were rearranged.

# Mining Islands
Aliases: Mining Island, Mining island, Mining islands
[Mining Islands]
Mining Islands are a category of [[Locations|Public Islands]] revolving around the [[Mining]] skill.
## Overview
All Mining Islands, unlike most other islands, use a custom mining system that utilises [[Mining Speed]]. To achieve this, the server grants the player permanent Mining Fatigue, which prevents players from breaking blocks that are not intended to be mined. This behaviour is also seen in locations such as [[The End]], the [[Backwater Bayou]], and the [[Catacombs]].
## List of Mining Islands
### Gold Mine
The Gold Mine
The [[Gold Mine]] is the first Mining Island, accessible at [[Mining|Mining I (1)]] from the [[Mining District]] and [[Coal Mine]]. Players can mine [[Coal]], [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]], and [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]].
Additionally, the Gold Mine contains 3 major attractions:
- [[Rusty]] allows the player to re-purchase one-time rewards for [[Coins]].
- The [[Lazy Miner]] sends the player on a quest to collect his [[Iron Pickaxe]].
- The [[Iron Forger]] and [[Gold Forger]] sell iron and gold gear.
### Deep Caverns
The Deep Caverns
The [[Deep Caverns]] is the second Mining Island, consisting of 6 layers: the [[Gunpowder Mines]], [[Lapis Quarry]], [[Pigmen's Den]], [[Slimehill]], [[Diamond Reserve]], and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]. Each section is connected by the [[Lift Operator]], which also allows access to the [[Dwarven Mines]].
Each section has its corresponding ore and mob:
- [[Sneaky Creeper|Sneaky Creepers]] can be found in the [[Gunpowder Mines]], where [[Coal]], [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]], and [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]] can be mined.
- [[Lapis Zombie|Lapis Zombies]] can be found in the [[Lapis Quarry]], where [[Lapis Lazuli]] can be mined.
- [[Redstone Pigman|Redstone Pigmen]] can be found in the [[Pigmen's Den]], where [[Redstone Dust]] can be mined.
- [[Slime (disambiguation)|Slimes]] can be found in the [[Slimehill]], where [[Emerald|Emeralds]] can be mined.
- [[Sneaky Creeper|Sneaky Creepers]] can be found in the [[Gunpowder Mines]], where [[Coal]], [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]], and [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]] can be mined.
- [[Miner Zombie|Miner Zombies]] and [[Miner Skeleton|Miner Skeletons]] can be found in the [[Diamond Reserve]], where [[Diamond|Diamonds]] can be mined.
  - Stronger variants appear in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]], where [[Obsidian]] can be mined.
### Dwarven Mines
The [[Dwarven Mines]] are a location where the player unlocks the [[Heart of the Mountain]].
### Crystal Hollows
The [[Crystal Hollows]] can be accessed after reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain Tier 4]] from the [[Dwarven Mines]].
- In the Crystal Hollows, the player can mine [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] and complete the [[Crystal Nucleus]].

|[[Hunter Knife]]|[[Hunter Knife]]|[[Potato Minion]]|[[Potato]]|[[French Fries]]|Complete the [[Woods Race]] in 1 minute, after that it's available at [[Rusty]] for 500k [[Coins]]|
These upgrades have a chance to generate an item for every item that the minion harvests. The spreading effect triggers from the base drops of the minion. While the island is loaded, the spreading effect also triggers from the [[Egg]] made at spawn of a chicken from a [[Chicken Minion]] with [[Enchanted Egg]]. While the island is unloaded, the spreading effect also triggers from drops of [[Enchanted Egg]], [[Corrupt Soil]], and [[Enchanted Shears]]. While the island is unloaded, the spreading effect triggers only once in an [[Inferno Minion]], even if the used [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] makes more than one drop.
|Icon|Name|Restricted to|Spreading Effect Chance per drop|Spreading Effect Generated item|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Diamond Spreading]]|[[Diamond Spreading]]||0.1 (1 in 10)|[[Diamond]]|Crafting (requires Diamond VI (6) ([[Diamond]])) Recipe: [[Vines]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] → [[Diamond Spreading]]|
|[[Potato Spreading]]|[[Potato Spreading]]|one of any minion|0.05 (1 in 20)|[[Potato]]|1.45% (1 in 69.03 pigs) from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])|
|[[Sleepy Hollow]]|[[Sleepy Hollow]]|one of any minion|0.00015 (1 in 6666.67)|[[Purple Candy]]|Purchased from [[Vargul the Unearthed]] for 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Dark Candy]] during the Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]])|
|[[Krampus Helmet]]|[[Krampus Helmet]]|one of any minion|0.000045 (1 in 22222.22)|[[Red Gift]]|0.10% (1 in 969.12 gifts) from Red Gifts ([[Red Gift]])|
These upgrades generates an item on a cooldown, that is independent of the minion speed. While online, the item will only be generated on a harvest after the cooldown has passed. The cooldown doesn't restart until the item is generated, so while the time between generations isn't affected by Minion Speed, it still relies on it.
|Icon|Name|Restricted to|Cooldown Effect Cooldown|Cooldown Effect Generated item|Other effects|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lesser Soulflow Engine]]|[[Lesser Soulflow Engine]]||180 seconds,; 185 seconds while offline|[[Raw Soulflow]]|Reduce the minion's output by 50%.|Crafting (requires Enderman Slayer II (2) ([[Enderman Slayer]])) Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] ×6 + [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] x24 ×2 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] x8 → [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]]|
|[[Soulflow Engine]]|[[Soulflow Engine]]||90 seconds,; 105 seconds while offline|[[Raw Soulflow]]|Reduce the minion's output by 50%. When placed in a [[Voidling Minion]], the [[Raw Soulflow]] output is increased by 3% per minion tier.|Crafting (requires Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])) Recipe: [[Null Atom]] ×2 + [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]] ×4 + [[Null Ovoid]] x64 + [[Refined Titanium]] x6 ×2 → [[Soulflow Engine]]|
|[[Berberis Fuel Injector]]|[[Berberis Fuel Injector]]|Farming Minions (Wheat, Melon, Pumpkin, Carrot, Potato, Mushroom, Cactus, Cocoa Beans, Sugar Cane, Nether Wart, Sunflower, Cow, Pig, Chicken, Sheep, Rabbit)|5 minutes|[[Lush Berberis]]|+15% minion speed.|Crafting (requires Wilted Berberis IV (4) ([[Wilted Berberis]])) Recipe: [[Wilted Berberis]] x48 ×7 → [[Berberis Fuel Injector]]|
|[[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|[[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|one Farming Minion|3 hours|[[Jacob's Ticket]]||Crafting Recipe: [[Chirping Stereo]] ×2 + [[PlantBoy Advance]] + [[Botroot]] x32 ×2 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x32 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×3 → [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|
These upgrades will automatically craft the drops into more compact forms.
|Icon|Name|Restricted to|Compacted Drops|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Compactor]]|[[Compactor]]|Various (Glowstone, Clay, Ice, Snow, Coal, Iron, Gold, Diamond, Lapis, Redstone, Emerald, Quartz, Melon, Mushroom, Slime)|Compacts items to their block forms. Example:; 9 [[Iron Ingot]] → [[Block of Iron]]; 4 [[Glowstone Dust]] → [[Glowstone]]|Crafting (requires Cobblestone V (5) ([[Cobblestone]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] → [[Compactor]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|Super; Compactor 3000 ([[Super Compactor 3000]])||Compacts items to their enchanted forms. Also compacts second-tier enchanted items when there are enough resources. Example:; 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Iron Ingot]] → [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]; 160 [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] → [[Enchanted Glowstone]]|Crafting (requires Cobblestone X (10) ([[Cobblestone]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x32 ×7 + [[Compactor]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x32 → [[Super Compactor 3000]]|
|[[Dwarven Super Compactor]]|[[Dwarven Super Compactor]]||Automatically smelts and compacts items into their enchanted forms. Combines the functionalities of the [[Auto Smelter]] and the [[Super Compactor 3000]].|Crafting (requires Mithril VI (6) ([[Mithril]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x8 ×7 + [[Auto Smelter]] + [[Super Compactor 3000]] → [[Dwarven Super Compactor]]|
These upgrades do not fit in the previous categories, but do have an effect on the minions.
|Icon|Name|Restricted to|Effect|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minion Expander]]|[[Minion Expander]]||Increases the minion range by one block (7x7 Area, 1 block each side) and increases minion speed by +5%. Placing two in the same minion will cause the effects to stack and will result in a minion range of 9x9.|Crafting (requires Nether Quartz V (5) ([[Nether Quartz]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x2 → [[Minion Expander]]|
|[[Flycatcher]]|[[Flycatcher]]||Increases the speed of a minion by +20%.|Crafting (requires Spider Slayer VI (6) ([[Spider Slayer]])) Recipe: [[Tarantula Silk]] x17 ×6 + [[Fly Swatter]] → [[Flycatcher]]|
|[[Enchanted Egg]]|[[Enchanted Egg]]|[[Chicken Minion]]|Makes the Chickens ([[Chicken]]) lay an [[Egg]] after they spawn, and also adds an [[Egg]] to the drops of the Chickens ([[Chicken]]). This results in two Eggs ([[Egg]]) being made in one cycle while the island is loaded, one on spawn and one on harvest. While the island is unloaded, only one [[Egg]] will be made per cycle.|Crafting (requires Raw Chicken V (5) ([[Raw Chicken]])) Recipe: [[Egg]] x16 ×9 → [[Enchanted Egg]]|
|[[Corrupt Soil]]|[[Corrupt Soil]]|Mob-spawning Minions (Zombie, Revenant, Voidling, Inferno, Vampire, Skeleton, Creeper, Spider, Tarantula, Cave Spider, Blaze, Magma Cube, Enderman, Ghast, Slime, Cow, Pig, Chicken, Sheep, Rabbit)|Make minions spawn [[Corrupted Mobs]], which adds [[Sulphur]] and [[Corrupted Fragment]] to the drops of the mob.; While online, the following minions can make multiple [[Sulphur]] and Corrupted Fragments ([[Corrupted Fragment]]) per harvest: [[Revenant Minion]], [[Voidling Minion]], [[Skeleton Minion]], [[Tarantula Minion]], [[Magma Cube Minion]], [[Slime Minion]].; [[Inferno Minion]] is not able to make [[Sulphur]] and [[Corrupted Fragment]] while online.|Crafting (requires Mycelium VI (6) ([[Mycelium]])) Recipe: [[Enchanted Mycelium]] x20 ×8 + [[Corrupted Fragment]] x20 → [[Corrupt Soil]]|
|[[Enchanted Shears]]|[[Enchanted Shears]]|[[Sheep Minion]]|Shears the sheep after they spawn, which gives 1–3 [[Wool]], averaging to 2 [[Wool]] per sheep. Sheared sheep do not drop [[Wool]] when killed.|Crafting Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] ×2 → [[Enchanted Shears]]|

# Mob Types/List/Elusive
|Mob Name|Mob Types|Source|
|-|-|-|
|[[Golden Goblin]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Humanoid]]; [[Shielded]]|Has a chance to spawn when mining certain blocks in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]] or throwing Goblin Eggs ([[Goblin Egg]]).; Spawn rate is not affected by [[Tracking]].|
|[[Diamond Goblin]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Humanoid]]; [[Shielded]]|Has a chance to replace a [[Golden Goblin]].|
|Elusive Trapper Mobs ([[Trevor]])|[[Elusive]]; [[Animal]]|When farming Pelts via talking to [[Trevor]], there is a small chance(0.99%) for a mob to spawn in as an Elusive mob.|
|[[Field Mouse]]|[[Elusive]]; Pest ([[Pest (Mob Type)]])|When a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]], or [[Vermin Trap]] fills, a Field Mouse has a chance to be the pest that appears.|
|Golden Jerry ([[Hidden Jerry]])|[[Elusive]]; [[Humanoid]]; [[Shielded]]|Has a chance to spawn while Jerry ([[Candidate Jerry]]) is mayor.|
|[[Sphinx]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Humanoid]]; [[Mythological]]|Has a chance to be dug up from [[Griffin Burrows]] with LEGENDARY and above [[Griffin Pet]].|
|[[Minos Inquisitor]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Humanoid]]; [[Mythological]]|Has a chance to be dug up from [[Griffin Burrows]] with LEGENDARY and above [[Griffin Pet]].|
|[[King Minos]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Mythological]]; [[Humanoid]]|Has a chance to be dug up from [[Griffin Burrows]] with MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Griffin Pet]].|
|[[Manticore]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Animal]]; [[Mythological]]|Has a chance to be dug up from [[Griffin Burrows]] with MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Griffin Pet]].|
|[[Scatha]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Shielded]]; [[Subterranean]]|Has a chance to replace a [[Stoneworm]].|
|[[Special Zealot]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Ender]]|Has a chance to spawn after a [[Zealot]] or [[Zealot Bruiser]] is killed.|
|[[Vanquisher]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Wither]]|Has a chance to spawn after any mob is killed in the [[Crimson Isle]].|
|[[Littlefoot]]|[[Elusive]]; Glacial ([[Glacial (Mob Type)]]); [[Humanoid]]|Has a chance to replace any mob in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].|
|[[Flipflopper]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Aquatic]]; [[Animal]]|Has a chance to spawn from Exploding Lily Pads ([[Lotus Atoll]]).|
|[[Seashine]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Aquatic]]; [[Animal]]|Has a chance to spawn from Exploding Lily Pads ([[Lotus Atoll]]).|
|[[Grizzly Bear]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Woodland]]; [[Animal]]|Has a chance to spawn from Helix Tree Gifts ([[Tree Gifts]]).|
### Sea Creatures
Tracking multiplicatively increases the [[Elusive]] Sea Creatures [[Sea Creatures|Weight]]. For example, if you had 5 [[Tracking]], that would be a 1.05x multiplier. Every [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] and [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] has an [[Elusive]] mob type.
|Mob Name|Mob Types|Source|
|-|-|-|
|[[Water Hydra]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Animal]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water.|
|[[Phantom Fisher]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Spooky]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water one hour before, one hour after and during [[Spooky Festival]].|
|[[Grim Reaper]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Spooky]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water one hour before, one hour after and during [[Spooky Festival]].|
|[[Great White Shark]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Animal]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water during [[Fishing Festival]].|

|[[Reindrake]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Airborne]]; Frozen ([[Frozen (Mob Type)]]); [[Shielded]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water in the [[Jerry's Workshop]].|
|[[Abyssal Miner]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Subterranean]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water in the [[Jungle]], [[Mithril Deposits]] or [[Precursor Remnants]].|

|[[Silkbreeze]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Aquatic]]; [[Airborne]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].|
|[[Giant Isopod]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Aquatic]]; [[Arthropod]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].|
|[[Puddle Jumper]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Animal]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water and spawned from Exploding Lily Pads ([[Lotus Atoll]]) in the [[Lotus Atoll]].|
|[[Frog Prince]]|[[Elusive]]; [[Animal]]; [[Aquatic]]|Has a chance to be fished up from water and spawned from Exploding Lily Pads ([[Lotus Atoll]]) in the [[Lotus Atoll]].|
